## Read contract (all DTO fields **new** unless the seam above names them)

`GET /api/sites/{site_code}/overview?date=YYYY-MM-DD` selects one UTC day. With no date or range, imported Sites default to the DB clock's current UTC day and return that day in `window.utc_date`; the UI writes it to the URL for reload. Accept existing `from`/`to` only when exactly one UTC day; reject partial, conflicting or non-UTC selection with typed 422. For legacy Sites, translate `date=` into the same midnight `from`/`to` pair and retain the existing file-backed DTO; with no selection retain its existing `LATEST_RELEASE` default. Return versioned `SiteWindowSummary` for imported Sites:

| Field | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `schema_version, site_id, site_code, access_basis, window {start,end,utc_date}, built_at, db_now` | Exact Site/day, configured development access basis and DB-time basis. |
| `freshness {state,stage,reasons,errors,source_open,source_reason}` | `UNASSESSED` (UI Unknown), `PENDING`, `STALE_PROJECTION`, `FAILED`, `CURRENT`; carry typed S3/schedule/work reasons. Closed source cannot be CURRENT. `S3_UNASSESSED` is a gap, never success. |
| `watermarks {accepted_source,analytic_selection_seq,input_sha256,configuration_sha256,match_pass_id,matched_through_selection_seq}, model_version` | Accepted ledger/source and exact analytic/match basis. `accepted_source` is a **new** deterministic fingerprint and count of accepted ledger IDs intersecting the selected day plus S3's six-hour query bracket, ordered by stable ID; compare it before/after read. Missing ledger access gives null plus reason and blocks CURRENT. Never use heartbeat/disposition time as the fence. |
| `assessment {run_id,window_assessment_revision_id,status,claim_level,limitations,coverage,dispatch_intervals,links}` | One selected S3 day; unassessed objective is Unknown. Timeline cites the selected assessment and accepted evidence; do not recalculate dispatch. |
| `context {series,coverage,gaps,evidence_links}` | Accepted measurements with time/unit/signal/evidence ID and quality limit. Missing/conflicting inputs give null and a reason. |
| `money {state,amount_low,amount_high,currency,model_version,finding_id,finding_revision_id,consequence_revision_id,eligibility,quantity_state,price_state,limitation,demonstration,links}` | One Finding only. Amount low is zero and upper is bounded only for exact-head, same FindingRevision/window/basis `CURRENT PRICED`, open source, upstream CURRENT and shared `ELIGIBLE`. Both amount fields use the outward-rounded `display` values from that same consequence revision; its unrounded `amount` remains exact drill-down material. `MINOR_UNIT_UNKNOWN` gives no displayed amount. Otherwise both fields are null with limiting-state links. |
| `known_finding {id,revision_id,selection_rule,link}, finding_list {state:"UNAVAILABLE",reason}` | A known selected Finding can link directly; no list rows/count/empty claim. |

Architect-ruled Finding choice (`D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`): discover the latest recorded FindingRevision of each tenant/Site-scoped canonical Finding in the same fenced S3/S4 basis. Include only episodes with half-open intersection `episode_start < day_end and episode_end > day_start`; an episode ending exactly at `00:00Z` on the selected day is excluded. The S4 `findings_over` bracket read (`backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:418-431`) uses a wider bracket and a closed lower-end test, so explicitly re-filter and exclude IDs superseded by an S4 merge. Do not filter by lifecycle, consequence state, price, amount or money eligibility. Choose greatest `episode_start`, then lexicographically smallest stable Finding UUID. Return the rule, Finding ID and exact revision ID. If several intersect, say the card covers this Finding only. Keep that pinned Finding and exact links through pending, stale, withheld, unpriced or other unavailable money. A changed, consistently selected S4 episode basis may change the choice; an incomplete or moving basis retains the previous pinned choice as stale/pending without current amount. `ABSENT` is allowed only after complete, current scoped discovery finds no intersecting canonical Finding.

S5 no-amount states (**new DTO vocabulary**): `ABSENT`, `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, `SOURCE_CLOSED`, `UPSTREAM_UNASSESSED`, `UPSTREAM_PENDING`, `UPSTREAM_STALE`, `UPSTREAM_FAILED`, `SUPERSEDED`, `CONSEQUENCE_PENDING`, `CONSEQUENCE_STALE`, `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE`, `UNPRICED`, `MINOR_UNIT_UNKNOWN`, `MIXED_OR_UNKNOWN_SOURCE`. Preserve underlying LR eligibility, S4 currentness/reasons, work IDs and historical links. Precedence: source closed; upstream failed/stale/pending/unassessed (including `S3_UNASSESSED`); LR pending then withheld; superseded; consequence pending/stale; quantity unavailable; unpriced; unknown currency minor unit; mixed/unknown source; only then priced. `MIXED_OR_UNKNOWN_SOURCE` is produced when `demonstration_case` returns the unverified label because its fixture specification cannot be read (`backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:60-69`); if no other trusted provenance resolves that case, show no amount. A historical `CURRENT PRICED` row remains audit-only after series ambiguity. Reuse `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:60-76` and Finding bridge `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275,306` for authored P01. Site/day matching is a bounded fixture label, **not** stored authorship. Label the `fuel-cost-1` model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, zero lower bound and assumption/evidence lineage; never savings or a physical maximum.

The S5 amount read must use the consequence revision whose ID equals the selected FindingRevision's seam `head_consequence_revision_id` (`backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:155-161` on LR-01). If a later exact read observes a different head, or its FindingRevision/window/basis changed, fail closed as stale/pending and retry on a new request; never combine an old seam verdict with a newer amount.

# Architect answer - Site FindingIndex (2026-10-15)

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Resolve `A-finding-index` for the W1 Site read only. Authority is the owner-absence work order item 9, the reviewed S4-01 episode contract, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`. This decision defines a reusable row contract; it does not implement an estate/global index, cold-chain or EV analytics, or approve UI copy.

## Scope and row selection

- Require an authenticated tenant, resolved Site UUID/code, domain and selected half-open UTC window `[from,to)` of at most 31 UTC days. Reject longer windows. The W1 producer is mini-grid dispatch. Resolve the Site within the tenant and use the established 401/403/404 identity rules. Do not silently expand to another Site, another domain or the mock's global Findings area. Echo the requested window and display timezone; changing dates makes a new scoped read, not a client-side filter of an old list.
- Start with persisted S4-01 Finding and immutable FindingRevision records, never a UI seed, S3 interval alone, Lab private state or a financial consequence. Select the latest permitted revision of each canonical Site Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis. A row qualifies only when its selected episode intersects the window (`episode_start < to` and `episode_end > from`); a boundary touch does not qualify. Apply S4 merge canonicalization: one row for the surviving ID, with superseded IDs available as relationship/history links. Extension changes the revision on the same Finding; separated recurrence uses a new Finding ID and recurrence link. Closed and unconfirmed episodes remain eligible historical rows in an intersecting window; the default list is not an implicit open-only filter.
- Row identity is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)` with the stable Finding UUID, canonical ID, exact selected `finding_revision_id`, domain, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort and cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs. Include limitations and exact Site detail URL `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`; the API may also return an unpinned canonical URL. The selected revision link must survive reload and later revision creation. A row's source and analytic lineage is the selected immutable revision's lineage, not a recomputation from its title or latest unrelated consequence.

## Ordering and pagination

- Do not infer a priority or confidence rank from the v6.9 illustrative rows. W1 default order is selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending. This replaces the provisional `(priority_tuple, updated_at, finding_id)` ordering in the pre-S3 guidance until a reviewed priority producer exists. A future reviewed priority rule may add a versioned `priority_tuple`; until then any priority/lens control without a supported producer is hidden or explicitly unavailable. Never order by mutable amount or arrival time. Return a deterministic total count and opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, domain, window, selected basis/watermark and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates the cursor and requires a fresh first page; it must not splice different revision sets together. Page size is bounded. S5-02 consumes the same ordered Site rows and does not independently rank or select an S5-01 card Finding.

## Watermark and currentness

- Return a read-level selection token/basis ID, accepted-source watermark, S3 assessment selection and analytic watermark, S4 matcher/selection watermark, model/matcher version, `built_at`, coverage/limitations and a typed status. A transactionally consistent scoped read or atomically published projection is acceptable. `knowledge_at` is the stated selection cutoff; do not promise arbitrary commit-ordered historical replay from the current S3 worker clock. Exact revision IDs are the audit anchors.
- `CURRENT` requires complete scoped discovery: every UTC-day analytic window intersected by `[from,to)`, plus neighbouring windows reachable through the S4-01 20-minute episode-continuity rule, must have S3 selection and canonical S4 matching through each window's current selection seq on the same accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis. No affected dirty, leased or failed S3/S4 work, watermark mismatch or moving selection is allowed. Relevant late evidence or changed configuration/model invalidates affected windows and must rebuild or make them stale. `PENDING` means no usable completed selection yet; `STALE_PROJECTION` retains prior rows/IDs with the reason while a newer basis is unresolved; `FAILED` reports failed work and any prior selected rows as historical. `UNASSESSED` means no completed assessment/discovery basis. None of these statuses authorizes a current count, current empty claim or an inferred Healthy state. A source-coverage limitation may coexist with a current, complete assessment of the available evidence; state the limitation instead of calling the physical world complete.
- Apply the Site's S3 Performance cutover source gate before any unpinned index read. If it is closed, refuse the index with HTTP 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` and the gate reason, matching the Finding routes. Return neither `CURRENT` rows nor a zero-row/no-Findings claim. Exact `revision_id` Finding detail remains the separate audit-only route. This rule also consumes the reviewed S4-05 stored provenance form of the gate once merged.
- Keep list freshness separate from each row's assessment claim and from money eligibility. A current list can include an `UNCONFIRMED` Finding or a row with no current amount. A stale list cannot silently replace its selected revision links with newer detail heads.

## Independent fixture proof

- `D-site-finding-index-empty`: persist a tenant/Site/domain/window with an accepted-source and S3/S4 selection watermark, complete assessed discovery, zero canonical intersecting rows and a `CURRENT` zero-row response. Prove a wrong Site/window is excluded and a boundary-touch episode is excluded. Separately exercise unassessed, dirty/stale and failed reads, each visibly Unknown/pending rather than a no-Findings assertion; close the S3 source gate and prove HTTP 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` with its reason and no current rows or zero claim. A filtered empty page after a positive unfiltered read is not the zero-row fixture.
- `D-site-finding-index-rows`: use committed accepted evidence and S4-01 revisions to persist at least one intersecting canonical Finding, ideally multiple for order and cursor proof. Verify stable ID, latest selected revision, exact detail/history link after reload, recurrence versus extension and canonical merge behavior, cross-Site isolation, date change, boundary intersection and invalidation after a late revision. Empty and positive fixtures are separate W1 closeouts; both must be reviewed. Do not count the mock's F-108 or F-109 as produced Findings without their own reviewed analytics.

## Money and provenance

- A row's existence, order, lifecycle and links are independent of money. The optional consequence cell may use only the exact selected FindingRevision and compatible selected-window consequence. Consume the shared LR-01 `money_eligibility` state and S4-05 exact accepted-ID publication provenance when those reviewed but currently unmerged branches become the build baseline. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, mixed/unknown source, stale list or consequence, wrong revision/window, unpriced quantity/price or unresolved source-open state means no current amount. Preserve an old value only behind an explicit historical/audit link. Coherent authored or registered simulation must carry its truthful label and still pass exact-head, currentness and quantity/price gates. Do not aggregate, sum overlapping Findings, infer savings, or turn missing price into zero. If S4-04 starts before those branches merge, leave the consequence cell unavailable and add the reviewed contracts before any current-money presentation or S5-02 integration.

## S4-04 and S5-02 handoff

S4-04 builds the authenticated Site/window index read or equivalent projection, affected-work invalidation, stable cursor and exact revision links, then the Site Findings route/list with selected scope, loading/stale/failed/empty/positive states and keyboard navigation. Add a new index read; do not change `findings_over` in place, since `scheduling/health.py`, `scheduling/upstream.py` and `analytics/finding_read.py` consume its inclusive-end and ascending-order semantics. Its selected revisions must share a consistent matcher/assessment fence; a helper alone is not proof of complete discovery. Compare the supported Site screen at desktop and narrow widths, but do not copy unsupported mock rows, global-list links, Cost/Risks/Opportunities filters, priority/confidence badges or open counts without real producers. The reviewer verifies both fixtures independently, same-basis pagination, tenant isolation, reload and exact drill-down. Test that on the same Site/window/basis the first index row's Finding and revision IDs equal the S5-01 card Finding and selected revision. Owner walkthrough and copy acceptance remain pending until performed.

S5-02 consumes this S4-04 contract on the S5-01 selected Site/window and preserves its own scope, watermark and exact row links. It proves `mini:3#a` honest empty/Unknown and `mini:3#b` positive rows independently. S5-01 may retain a known selected Finding card link but does not close either list variant. S4-03 Evidence/History, `mini:1/2/4` and S3 dispatch do not acquire this index as a prerequisite.
# A-finding-index - independent review (fresh Claude reviewer)

Date: 2026-10-15
Reviewed: branch `plan/A-finding-index`, HEAD `002f8cfe` ("A-finding-index resolved"), base `789d76d1`.
Inputs: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-request.md`, `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md`, `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index` (`.ai/DECISIONS.md`), registry diff, `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md`, `tasks/S5-02-overview-finding-list.md`, related decisions, v69 `sitefindings` mock, S4-01 code on this base, S5-01 read on `task/S5-01-site-overview` (via `git show`).

## Verdict: RETURN (one Medium; fix is a short contract amendment)

The contract is otherwise sound and implementable. One Medium fail-closed gap: the decision never applies the existing S3 performance-cutover source gate to the index rows/zero claim.

## Findings

### M1 (Medium, acceptance/contract gap) - S3 source gate missing from index currentness

- Where: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md:18` (currentness) and `:28` (money); `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`.
- What: "source-open" appears only as a money gate ("unresolved source-open state means no current amount"). The currentness rules for `CURRENT` (complete discovery, no dirty/leased/failed work, no watermark mismatch) do not mention the Site's S3 performance-cutover gate.
- Why it matters: on this base `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:86-90,104-107,116-117` refuses with 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` when the gate is closed: "no current Finding or no-Finding verdict is shown. An exact revision_id reads audit material." The S5-01 read on `task/S5-01-site-overview` (`site_overview/read.py`) does the same: it drops to `STALE_PROJECTION`, chooses no unpinned Finding (`SOURCE_CLOSED_NO_UNPINNED_FINDING`) and allows `ABSENT` only on an open source. As written, an S4-04 implementer can return `CURRENT` positive rows, or a `CURRENT` zero-row "no Findings", for a closed-source Site. That contradicts Finding detail, which would then 409 or show audit-only, and contradicts S5-01 on the same Site/day. S5-02 would then show list rows next to an S5-01 card that says no current Finding.
- Fix: add one rule to the answers file and the decision. A closed S3 source gate (whatever reviewed form it takes, including S4-05 stored provenance once merged) forbids `CURRENT` and any zero-row claim. Either refuse the way the existing Finding routes do (409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED`), or return the rows as audit-only/historical with exact revision links and the gate's reason, and say which. Add a closed-gate variant to the `D-site-finding-index-empty` fixture list (alongside unassessed/dirty/failed) and the matching registry acceptance target.

### L1 (Low) - discovery fence for multi-day and boundary-crossing windows not pinned

- `answers:17-18`: "complete scoped discovery" is not tied to a concrete set of analytic windows. On this base, completeness is per UTC-day analytic window (`postgres_finding_store.match_state`: `analytic_run_selection.seq` vs `finding_match_pass.selection_seq`). An episode that intersects `[from,to)` can be anchored in an earlier day through the 20-minute continuity bridge. The Planner should require every intersecting UTC-day window and its continuity neighbours to be matched through their current selection seq (as LR-01 requeue scope already does) before `CURRENT`. The Planner should also bound the selectable window length, since page size is bounded but window span is not.

### L2 (Low) - "reconcile `findings_over`" could break existing consumers

- `answers:32`: the helper's inclusive end and ascending order are correctly identified as non-conforming (`postgres_finding_store.py:418-430`). It is also consumed by `scheduling/health.py:252`, `scheduling/upstream.py:133` and `analytics/finding_read.py:617`, which S5-01 extends. The S4-04 packet should add a new index read, or prove those consumers unaffected, rather than change the helper's semantics in place.

### L3 (Low) - S5-01 card and index head should provably agree

- Ordering (episode_start desc, Finding UUID asc) matches `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice` and S5-01's `choose()` (greatest start, smallest `str(uuid)`). PostgreSQL uuid byte order equals lowercase-hex string order, so they agree. Recommend that S4-04/S5-02 include a test showing that, for the same basis, the first index row equals the S5-01 card Finding.

### L4 (Low, housekeeping) - stale planning text and anchor style

- `tasks/README.md:9` still says `A-finding-index` "remain[s] open", and `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md:14` still says "Decide `A-finding-index`". The Planner should refresh both at S4-04 refinement.
- The registry evidence uses the lowercase anchor `#d-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, while sibling nodes use `#D-...`. Cosmetic only, since decisions are not headings.

## What was verified

- Implementability on this base: `finding`/`finding_revision` (append-only, `revision_no`, `episode_start <= episode_end`), `finding_merge` (superseded -> canonical), `finding_match_pass` and `analytic_run_selection` seq exist (`0009_finding_store.py`, `postgres_finding_store.py:400-416`). Canonicalization, latest revision selection, the per-window matcher watermark and the strict half-open overlap can all be built without new architecture. The exact detail URL with `revision_id` already exists (`finding_api.py:110-135`).
- Fail-closed honesty: apart from M1, a zero-row claim requires complete current discovery. PENDING/STALE_PROJECTION/FAILED/UNASSESSED never authorize a count or empty claim. Positive rows come only from persisted immutable revisions, with mock F-108/F-109 excluded. A stale list keeps its selected revision links.
- Stable IDs and ordering: the row key is `(tenant, site, finding_id)`; there is no ordering by money or arrival time; the cursor is bound to the basis and invalidated on change; the provisional priority tuple is withdrawn until a producer exists.
- Consistency: matches `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice` (half-open, canonical-only, no lifecycle/money filter, same tie-break) and `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview` (mini:3 belongs to S4-04/S5-02). LR-01 `money_eligibility` and S4-05 exact-revision provenance are consumed only for the optional amount cell. If S4-04 starts before those merge, the cell stays unavailable. Mixed/unknown source and withheld/pending eligibility show no current amount, consistent with `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort` and `D-2026-10-15-s405-stored-demo-provenance`.

# Backend testing guide

How to run the backend suite, when to run all of it, and how to prove a full
run was real. Database setup itself is in [README-db.md](README-db.md).

## When to run what

- **While building a phase:** run the focused files for the area you changed,
  plus adjacent integration tests. When an interface crosses into the host or
  gateway, also run those suites.
- **At the end of an ordinary slice:** one real-Postgres full backend suite,
  on the slice's final revision, with the JUnit zero-skip check below. Cite it
  in the review packet.
- **Reviewers** run the focused files relevant to their findings and cite the
  implementer's same-revision full evidence. A reviewer reruns the full suite
  only for a finding that needs it.
- **One full suite at a time on this machine.** Concurrent full runs were not
  attempted and are not proven safe; a full run is disk-bound, so a second one
  (or another Postgres-heavy agent) slows both and makes timing meaningless.
- OPS-01's baseline and comparison runs are the one documented exception to
  "one full suite per slice".
- `tools/check-all.ps1` is a separate required guard command; it does not run
  pytest, and pytest does not replace it.

## The test database

`ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` names a base database whose name ends in `_test`
(Compose creates `assetops_test`; agents may use a private one such as
`assetops_<task>_test`). Without it every Postgres module is reported as
**skipped**, and such a run is not valid full-suite proof.

The base database is used only as an administrative connection. At
configuration time `tests/conftest.py` calls `pg_harness.route()`, which
rewrites `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` to a run-owned name before any test module
is imported:

```
<base without _test>_pt<run>_<tag>_test     tag = main (serial), gw0, gw1... (xdist workers), tpl (template)
```

On first use the harness migrates the template to head once, then every
Postgres module's `engine` fixture drops the run's worker database `WITH
(FORCE)` and clones it from the template (`CREATE DATABASE ... TEMPLATE`), and
asserts the clone is at the Alembic head. Each module therefore still starts on
a fresh, fully migrated schema; migration up/downgrade tests still run in place
on that module's clone. `store` fixtures yield and dispose their engine.

Between tests the S1 `store` fixture empties the same tables
`TRUNCATE foundation_revision, source, gateway, asset, site_import, site, tenant
CASCADE` would, but with `DELETE` under `session_replication_role = replica`
(`pg_harness.empty_tables`). TRUNCATE rebuilds every index and on PostgreSQL 16
each rebuild forces a data-file sync, which Docker Desktop makes slow and
erratic (measured 1.4 s quiet, 22-25 s under disk pressure, per test; DELETE
0.14 s). `test_pg_harness.py` proves the DELETE set equals the tables TRUNCATE
CASCADE reports. `ASSETOPS_TEST_CLEANUP=truncate` restores TRUNCATE.

- In parallel (`-n 2`) the xdist controller runs no tests. It makes the run
  id and passes it to each worker; each worker routes itself to
  `..._pt<run>_gw<N>_test` before it imports a test module. The first worker
  that needs the template builds it under an advisory lock and the others reuse
  it. Workers drop only their own database; the controller drops the template
  and anything else the run left (a crashed worker's database) at the end.
  Under `--dist loadfile` every module stays on one worker, so module-scoped
  fixtures behave as serially. With `--no-loadscope-reorder` modules are handed
  out in collection order, and workers move the two long S4 Finding modules
  (`conftest.START_FIRST`) to the front so they start first; serial order is
  unchanged.
- Parametrize IDs must not be random: xdist refuses to run when workers collect
  different node IDs (`Different tests were collected between gw0 and gw1`).
- Nothing is provisioned for `--collect-only`, for a selection without Postgres
  modules, or with the URL unset.
- At the end of the session the run drops its own databases. A killed run's
  databases are swept by the next run with the same base: it drops
  `<base>_pt*` databases only when their run holds no live session (each run
  keeps one lease session whose `application_name` is
  `assetops-test-run:<run>`).
- `ASSETOPS_TEST_TEMPLATE=0` turns routing off and restores the old behaviour
  (drop `public` and migrate in place per module). Use it only if cloning is
  unavailable; it is serial-only and slower.
- Measurement knobs, off by default: `ASSETOPS_TEST_CLONE_STRATEGY`
  (`WAL_LOG` or `FILE_COPY`) and `ASSETOPS_TEST_SYNCHRONOUS_COMMIT` (applied with
  `ALTER DATABASE` to run-owned databases only). Server-wide settings are never
  changed by the tests.

## Commands (PowerShell, from the repository root)

```powershell
$env:ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL = "postgresql+psycopg://assetops:assetops-local-dev@127.0.0.1:55432/assetops_test"
$py = ".venv\Scripts\python.exe"

# S1 Foundation and bindings
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s1_foundation_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s1_bindings_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s1_cutover.py backend/tests/test_s1_bindings.py

# S2 ingestion and evidence
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s2_evidence_ledger_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_gateway_access_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_site_evidence_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_registered_basis_postgres.py

# S3 dispatch
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_read_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s3_demo_configuration.py

# S4 Finding and money
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_read_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_evidence_history_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_assumptions_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_recompute_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_consequence_work_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_drain_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_power_demo_case_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_tick_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_health_postgres.py

# Complete suite - the normal full command (two workers), with JUnit for the zero-skip proof
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml

# Complete suite, serial (fallback; also what a serial re-check of one file uses)
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml
```

### Before a long full run

Start the keep-awake helper in a separate window (or as a background job). It
asks Windows not to idle-sleep while any pytest process runs, changes no power
settings, and exits after 20 idle minutes. A machine that sleeps mid-run turns
a 40-minute suite into hours (seen in OPS-01: one run reached 34% in 4 h).

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\keep-awake-during-tests.ps1
```

Cap every full run at twice its expected wall time (see Timing below). A run
past the cap is reported as a hang, with the node ID it is stuck on (from
`pg_stat_activity` and the pytest log), not waited on.

### Zero-skip proof (from JUnit, not the summary line)

```powershell
[xml]$j = Get-Content .pytest-junit.xml
$cases = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase")
$skipped = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[skipped]")
$failed = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[failure or error]")
"cases=$($cases.Count) skipped=$($skipped.Count) failed=$($failed.Count)"
$skipped | ForEach-Object { "$($_.classname)::$($_.name) - $($_.skipped.message)" }
```

A full run is valid proof only when `skipped` is 0 and `failed` is 0. A skip
whose message names `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` means the database was unset.

## Timing

Measured on this Windows host (Docker Desktop Postgres 16), serial, full
suite. Run-to-run variance is large (disk sync inside Docker Desktop), so
compare only back-to-back runs in a quiet window.

| Revision | Runs (wall) | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Before OPS-01 (in-place migrate per module, TRUNCATE per test) | 80.3 min, 41.4 min; peak 91 sessions | 2 timing-sensitive tick failures in the 80-min run; a third attempt reached 34% in 4 h while the machine slept |
| Serial template clone + DELETE cleanup (OPS-01 phase 1) | 30.8 min (back-to-back with the 41.4-min baseline); peak 22 sessions | template clone without DELETE cleanup: 41.4 min, one 23-min disk stall in a tick test |
| `-n 2 --dist loadfile` (OPS-01 phase 2, normal command) | 7.7 min, 8.4 min; peak 35 and 32 sessions | 2128 passed, 0 skipped, 0 failed both times; node IDs identical to serial collection |
| `-n 3 --dist loadfile` (OPS-01 phase 2, one run) | 6.5 min; peak 41 sessions | 2128 passed; not repeated, so not the default |

Expected `-n 2` full run: about 8-10 min; cap at 20 min. Expected serial full
run: about 30-45 min; cap at 90 min. Use `-n 2`, never `-n auto`: the suite is
disk-bound inside Docker Desktop, and each worker adds about 10-20 sessions
against `max_connections = 100`.

If a timing-sensitive test (S4 tick hung-stage and claims replay, health skew
and overlap race, drain 30 s deadline, work enqueue-lock poll) fails under
`-n 2`, do not retry it: record the node ID and its duration, rerun that file
serially as a separate invocation, and report both runs' JUnit counts.

## Verified seam and implementation choice

- `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:160,385-393` already exposes `GET /api/sites/{site_id}/overview?from=&to=` in the evidence router. Today `site_or_404` (`:209-225`) raises 409 `AWAITING_EVIDENCE_MIGRATION` for an imported Site via `backend/assetops_backend/sites/cutover.py:114-119`; the UI shows `AwaitingEvidenceMigration` (`frontend/src/sites/SiteOverview.tsx:93-95`). Only legacy Sites reach the file-backed `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/overview.py:575-640` model. `frontend/src/sites/siteOverviewClient.ts:138-164,197-263` validates that old shape. **New:** make this existing handler delegate imported Sites to the versioned S5 read service and keep its legacy branch; do not register another route at this path, which the first included evidence router would shadow. Do not reuse the old Findings array as a Site index.
- Gate and scope composition (**new**): in `main.py`, inject into the existing evidence router an S5 service, `registry.ledger_scope` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/cutover.py:107-111`), the same S2-02 `ImportedEvidenceReads.scope` cutover check (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:229-245`), the same `performance_gate` (`backend/assetops_backend/main.py:329-340`), and the Finding `Access` hook/`access_basis` convention (`backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:36-40,63-77`). Apply `Access` before tenant/Site discovery; return its 401/403, use `ledger_scope` for the exact tenant/Site IDs, and expose the configured `access_basis` in the S5 response. For imported Sites, the S2 comparison must pass before the Overview serves accepted context, timeline, or evidence links: when absent/failed return the existing 409 `AWAITING_EVIDENCE_MIGRATION` for the whole imported Overview. The Performance gate controls `freshness.source_open`, selected S3 objective/status and current Finding/money claims; when closed, keep only explicitly pinned audit material and typed unavailable/closed sections, with no CURRENT assessment or amount. When both gates open, the selected S3/accepted-evidence sections may be current if their own fences pass; money still requires its separate gates. A closed Performance gate does not bypass the S2 comparison. Test comparison absent, Performance closed with comparison passed, and both open on the same route, including tenant/Site and access denials.
- `SiteWindowSummary` and `site_window_projection` exist only in design (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:68`); no runtime table/class exists. Choose an **equivalent scoped read over committed public records**; no new table/migration. Create the S5 read service/DTO (**new**) and reuse `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/composition.py:45-56` `build_upstream_currentness` for one `UpstreamCurrentness` instance per process, disposed at shutdown. `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:75-82,87-202` provides DB time, S3 day, match, Finding/consequence work and schedule eligibility. Its consequence read passes `source_open=True` at line 140 on the LR-01 head: S5 must apply the real Performance source gate before any current claim. Fence the selected sequence, accepted-source basis and work before/after composition; if they move, return stale/pending, never mixed CURRENT. Derived reads need no write-side invalidation.
- LR-01 **(unmerged, stacked; citations checked at `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` head)** adds `backend/assetops_backend/financials/money_eligibility.py:12-23,39-44,170-186` (`MoneyEligibility.for_finding`; `ELIGIBLE`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`). `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:214-239` exposes the gate and gates `current_amount_shown`; `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:149-161,189-200` includes the same result per Finding. Consume it; do not build a UI-only predicate. `ELIGIBLE` alone is never amount authority. LR-01 owns source-time attestation, overlap, six-hour requeue and explicit-epoch limits; S5 shows the selected basis/limit and preserves old exact versions.
- Existing source gate pattern: `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:79-85,112-128,246-277` (closed source permits pinned audit material, no unpinned current claim). Reuse it in S5 (**new integration**). Exact destinations already exist: dispatch day `backend/assetops_backend/performance_api.py:88`, Finding and financial consequence `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:112,246,284`, assumption revision `backend/assetops_backend/financial_read_api.py:88`, evidence record `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:158`. UI links must carry exact revision IDs; add a route only where an exact destination is absent (**new**).

## Read contract (all DTO fields **new** unless the seam above names them)

`GET /api/sites/{site_code}/overview?date=YYYY-MM-DD` selects one UTC day. With no date or range, imported Sites default to the DB clock's current UTC day and return that day in `window.utc_date`; the UI writes it to the URL for reload. Accept existing `from`/`to` only when exactly one UTC day; reject partial, conflicting or non-UTC selection with typed 422. For legacy Sites, translate `date=` into the same midnight `from`/`to` pair and retain the existing file-backed DTO; with no selection retain its existing `LATEST_RELEASE` default. Return versioned `SiteWindowSummary` for imported Sites:

| Field | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `schema_version, site_id, site_code, access_basis, window {start,end,utc_date}, built_at, db_now` | Exact Site/day, configured development access basis and DB-time basis. |
| `freshness {state,stage,reasons,errors,source_open,source_reason}` | `UNASSESSED` (UI Unknown), `PENDING`, `STALE_PROJECTION`, `FAILED`, `CURRENT`; carry typed S3/schedule/work reasons. Closed source cannot be CURRENT. `S3_UNASSESSED` is a gap, never success. |
| `watermarks {accepted_source,analytic_selection_seq,input_sha256,configuration_sha256,match_pass_id,matched_through_selection_seq}, model_version` | Accepted ledger/source and exact analytic/match basis. `accepted_source` is a **new** deterministic fingerprint and count of accepted ledger IDs intersecting the selected day plus S3's six-hour query bracket, ordered by stable ID; compare it before/after read. Missing ledger access gives null plus reason and blocks CURRENT. Never use heartbeat/disposition time as the fence. |
| `assessment {run_id,window_assessment_revision_id,status,claim_level,limitations,coverage,dispatch_intervals,links}` | One selected S3 day; unassessed objective is Unknown. Timeline cites the selected assessment and accepted evidence; do not recalculate dispatch. |
| `context {series,coverage,gaps,evidence_links}` | Accepted measurements with time/unit/signal/evidence ID and quality limit. Missing/conflicting inputs give null and a reason. |
| `money {state,amount_low,amount_high,currency,model_version,finding_id,finding_revision_id,consequence_revision_id,eligibility,quantity_state,price_state,limitation,demonstration,links}` | One Finding only. Amount low is zero and upper is bounded only for exact-head, same FindingRevision/window/basis `CURRENT PRICED`, open source, upstream CURRENT and shared `ELIGIBLE`. Both amount fields use the outward-rounded `display` values from that same consequence revision; its unrounded `amount` remains exact drill-down material. `MINOR_UNIT_UNKNOWN` gives no displayed amount. Otherwise both fields are null with limiting-state links. |
| `known_finding {id,revision_id,selection_rule,link}, finding_list {state:"UNAVAILABLE",reason}` | A known selected Finding can link directly; no list rows/count/empty claim. |

Architect-ruled Finding choice (`D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`): discover the latest recorded FindingRevision of each tenant/Site-scoped canonical Finding in the same fenced S3/S4 basis. Include only episodes with half-open intersection `episode_start < day_end and episode_end > day_start`; an episode ending exactly at `00:00Z` on the selected day is excluded. The S4 `findings_over` bracket read (`backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:418-431`) uses a wider bracket and a closed lower-end test, so explicitly re-filter and exclude IDs superseded by an S4 merge. Do not filter by lifecycle, consequence state, price, amount or money eligibility. Choose greatest `episode_start`, then lexicographically smallest stable Finding UUID. Return the rule, Finding ID and exact revision ID. If several intersect, say the card covers this Finding only. Keep that pinned Finding and exact links through pending, stale, withheld, unpriced or other unavailable money. A changed, consistently selected S4 episode basis may change the choice; an incomplete or moving basis retains the previous pinned choice as stale/pending without current amount. `ABSENT` is allowed only after complete, current scoped discovery finds no intersecting canonical Finding.

S5 no-amount states (**new DTO vocabulary**): `ABSENT`, `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, `SOURCE_CLOSED`, `UPSTREAM_UNASSESSED`, `UPSTREAM_PENDING`, `UPSTREAM_STALE`, `UPSTREAM_FAILED`, `SUPERSEDED`, `CONSEQUENCE_PENDING`, `CONSEQUENCE_STALE`, `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE`, `UNPRICED`, `MINOR_UNIT_UNKNOWN`, `MIXED_OR_UNKNOWN_SOURCE`. Preserve underlying LR eligibility, S4 currentness/reasons, work IDs and historical links. Precedence: source closed; upstream failed/stale/pending/unassessed (including `S3_UNASSESSED`); LR pending then withheld; superseded; consequence pending/stale; quantity unavailable; unpriced; unknown currency minor unit; mixed/unknown source; only then priced. `MIXED_OR_UNKNOWN_SOURCE` is produced when `demonstration_case` returns the unverified label because its fixture specification cannot be read (`backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:60-69`); if no other trusted provenance resolves that case, show no amount. A historical `CURRENT PRICED` row remains audit-only after series ambiguity. Reuse `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:60-76` and Finding bridge `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275,306` for authored P01. Site/day matching is a bounded fixture label, **not** stored authorship. Label the `fuel-cost-1` model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, zero lower bound and assumption/evidence lineage; never savings or a physical maximum.

The S5 amount read must use the consequence revision whose ID equals the selected FindingRevision's seam `head_consequence_revision_id` (`backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:155-161` on LR-01). If a later exact read observes a different head, or its FindingRevision/window/basis changed, fail closed as stale/pending and retry on a new request; never combine an old seam verdict with a newer amount.

# Backend testing guide

How to run the backend suite, when to run all of it, and how to prove a full
run was real. Database setup itself is in [README-db.md](README-db.md).

## When to run what

- **While building a phase:** run the focused files for the area you changed,
  plus adjacent integration tests. When an interface crosses into the host or
  gateway, also run those suites.
- **At the end of an ordinary slice:** one real-Postgres full backend suite,
  on the slice's final revision, with the JUnit zero-skip check below. Cite it
  in the review packet.
- **Reviewers** run the focused files relevant to their findings and cite the
  implementer's same-revision full evidence. A reviewer reruns the full suite
  only for a finding that needs it.
- **One full suite at a time on this machine.** Concurrent full runs were not
  attempted and are not proven safe; a full run is disk-bound, so a second one
  (or another Postgres-heavy agent) slows both and makes timing meaningless.
- OPS-01's baseline and comparison runs are the one documented exception to
  "one full suite per slice".
- `tools/check-all.ps1` is a separate required guard command; it does not run
  pytest, and pytest does not replace it.

## The test database

`ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` names a base database whose name ends in `_test`
(Compose creates `assetops_test`; agents may use a private one such as
`assetops_<task>_test`). Without it every Postgres module is reported as
**skipped**, and such a run is not valid full-suite proof.


# S4 Finding and money
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_read_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_evidence_history_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_assumptions_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_recompute_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_consequence_work_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_drain_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_power_demo_case_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_tick_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_health_postgres.py

# Complete suite - the normal full command (two workers), with JUnit for the zero-skip proof
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml

# Complete suite, serial (fallback; also what a serial re-check of one file uses)
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml
```

### Before a long full run

Start the keep-awake helper in a separate window (or as a background job). It
asks Windows not to idle-sleep while any pytest process runs, changes no power
settings, and exits after 20 idle minutes. A machine that sleeps mid-run turns
a 40-minute suite into hours (seen in OPS-01: one run reached 34% in 4 h).

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\keep-awake-during-tests.ps1
```

Cap every full run at twice its expected wall time (see Timing below). A run
past the cap is reported as a hang, with the node ID it is stuck on (from
`pg_stat_activity` and the pytest log), not waited on.

### Zero-skip proof (from JUnit, not the summary line)

```powershell
[xml]$j = Get-Content .pytest-junit.xml
$cases = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase")
$skipped = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[skipped]")
$failed = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[failure or error]")
"cases=$($cases.Count) skipped=$($skipped.Count) failed=$($failed.Count)"
$skipped | ForEach-Object { "$($_.classname)::$($_.name) - $($_.skipped.message)" }
```

+# S4-04 — Site Finding index and list
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; build and review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — owner visual and copy acceptance before merge or registry promotion.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review.
+Branch: `task/S4-04-site-finding-index`, **stacked on the independently reviewed S5-01 head** while S5-01 is unmerged. Its same-basis Finding-choice read is needed for parity proof. S5-01 itself is stacked on unmerged LR-01; stacked code is not merged or owner accepted. Resolve any S5-01 High/Medium review finding and rebase on its final reviewed head.
+Depends on: merged S4-01 Finding store/read and S3 assessment; resolved `A-finding-index` (`D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`); reviewed S5-01 for parity. S4-03 is registry queue order, not a technical index gate. LR-01 and S4-05 gate only optional current money.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+`sitefindings:1#a` is a **watermarked assessed zero-row** Site/window list; `sitefindings:1#b` has **persisted canonical Finding rows** and exact revision drill-down. Review both fixtures independently. W1 covers mini-grid dispatch, not the global Findings area or other domains. Authority: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md` (including closed-gate fix), `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, `.agent/A-finding-index-review.md`, `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`, and registry nodes `I-finding-index`, `I-site-findings-ui`, `D-site-finding-index-empty`, `D-site-finding-index-rows`, `I-S4-04`, panel `sitefindings:1` (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:332`). `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:329-334,640-641` and `screen-manifest.json:633-650` guide layout/interaction; illustrative F-108/F-109 are not evidence.

+## Read contract (route, DTO and cursor are new)
+
+Add `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/finding-index?domain=mini-grid-dispatch&from=<UTC>&to=<UTC>&limit=<bounded>&cursor=<opaque>` (route spelling is an Architect question below). Resolve tenant/Site via Finding access before discovery: established 401/403/404 and no foreign count. Require explicit domain and UTC half-open `[from,to)`, `from < to`, maximum 31 UTC days; typed 422 otherwise. Echo Site, UTC window and display timezone. The UI persists Site/domain/dates in its URL; date change makes a new read. Server bounds page size.
+
+Return `schema_version`, Site/domain/window, display timezone/access basis, selection token, `knowledge_at`, `built_at`, typed status/reasons, coverage/limitations, watermarks, rows, bounded current `total_count` and `next_cursor`. Watermarks contain accepted-source marker, S3 selected assessment/run/input/configuration and analytic selection, S4 matcher/version/pass/selection. Each row has stable `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id)` key, canonical Finding UUID, exact `finding_revision_id`, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort, cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs, limitations, relation/history links and `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`. Exact links survive reload and newer revisions; lineage comes from the selected immutable revision.
+
+Use one transactionally consistent read or atomically published projection; `knowledge_at` is a selection cutoff, not arbitrary commit-ordered replay. **Complete discovery** means every UTC-day analytic window intersecting the request and neighbours reachable through S4's 20-minute continuity rule has S3 selection and canonical S4 match through its current selection seq on one accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis. Fence before/after read. Moving selection, affected dirty/leased/failed work, watermark mismatch, or late evidence/configuration/model change blocks `CURRENT`. A source-coverage limitation can coexist with a current assessment of available evidence, but cannot assert physical completeness.
+
+`PENDING` means no usable completed selection; `UNASSESSED` no completed discovery basis; `STALE_PROJECTION` keeps prior rows/IDs and a reason; `FAILED` reports failed work with prior rows historical. None authorizes current count, empty/Healthy assertion or swapping stale links for a new head. A closed S3 Performance source gate returns 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` with its reason and no rows/count/empty claim; exact pinned detail remains a separate audit route. Consume S4-05's reviewed stored gate form when merged.
+
+One row per canonical Finding; merged-away IDs remain relationship links. Extension changes its revision; separated recurrence gets another Finding ID and link. Include closed and unconfirmed episodes. Intersect iff `episode_start < to && episode_end > from`; boundary touch is excluded. Order selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending; never by money, lifecycle or arrival. Cursor binds tenant/Site/domain/window, basis/selection token and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates it with a typed restart-first-page response; pages cannot splice revision sets. No priority tuple until a producer is reviewed.
+
+Optional amount cell starts **unavailable**. On the stacked S5-01/LR-01 baseline, LR-01 `money_eligibility` is reviewed but S4-05 stored accepted-ID provenance is unmerged: show no current amount. After **both** reviewed contracts enter the baseline, separate review may allow amount only for the exact selected FindingRevision/window/basis and consequence head, open source, current list/consequence, eligible series, priced quantity/price and known source/display unit. Withheld/pending, mixed/unknown source, stale, wrong revision/window, unpriced or closed source means no current amount; old values use explicit audit links. No sum, savings inference or unpriced-as-zero. Money does not gate row/empty delivery.
+

+Owner: prepare, serve, open `/sites/{site_code}/findings?from=<empty-start>&to=<next-midnight>&domain=mini-grid-dispatch`; inspect watermark and assessed zero. Change date and reload. Run `late`, refresh old cursor (must restart), then reopen old pinned revision and new row. All five registry `task` fields already say `S4-04`, and nodes/panel remain open until reviewed evidence and owner acceptance.
+
+1. **Route and cursor error:** recommend separate `/finding-index` route and 409 `INDEX_BASIS_CHANGED` with fresh-first-page instruction, avoiding `/findings/{finding_id}` collision and changes to `findings_over`.
+2. **Historical rows without a persisted projection:** recommend returning stale rows only from a durable previous selected snapshot; otherwise `PENDING`/`UNASSESSED` with no rows. Never rebuild stale rows from newer heads.
+3. **Continuity-neighbour closure:** recommend bounded expansion from intersected UTC days until no 20-minute episode bridge reaches another day; record inspected days and seq in selection token. Define a typed failure if expansion exceeds the bound.

+## Read contract (route, DTO and cursor are new)
+
+Add `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/finding-index?domain=mini-grid-dispatch&from=<UTC>&to=<UTC>&limit=<bounded>&cursor=<opaque>` (route spelling is an Architect question below). Resolve tenant/Site via Finding access before discovery: established 401/403/404 and no foreign count. Require explicit domain and UTC half-open `[from,to)`, `from < to`, maximum 31 UTC days; typed 422 otherwise. Echo Site, UTC window and display timezone. The UI persists Site/domain/dates in its URL; date change makes a new read. Server bounds page size.
+
+Return `schema_version`, Site/domain/window, display timezone/access basis, selection token, `knowledge_at`, `built_at`, typed status/reasons, coverage/limitations, watermarks, rows, bounded current `total_count` and `next_cursor`. Watermarks contain accepted-source marker, S3 selected assessment/run/input/configuration and analytic selection, S4 matcher/version/pass/selection. Each row has stable `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id)` key, canonical Finding UUID, exact `finding_revision_id`, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort, cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs, limitations, relation/history links and `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`. Exact links survive reload and newer revisions; lineage comes from the selected immutable revision.
+
+Use one transactionally consistent read or atomically published projection; `knowledge_at` is a selection cutoff, not arbitrary commit-ordered replay. **Complete discovery** means every UTC-day analytic window intersecting the request and neighbours reachable through S4's 20-minute continuity rule has S3 selection and canonical S4 match through its current selection seq on one accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis. Fence before/after read. Moving selection, affected dirty/leased/failed work, watermark mismatch, or late evidence/configuration/model change blocks `CURRENT`. A source-coverage limitation can coexist with a current assessment of available evidence, but cannot assert physical completeness.
+
+`PENDING` means no usable completed selection; `UNASSESSED` no completed discovery basis; `STALE_PROJECTION` keeps prior rows/IDs and a reason; `FAILED` reports failed work with prior rows historical. None authorizes current count, empty/Healthy assertion or swapping stale links for a new head. A closed S3 Performance source gate returns 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` with its reason and no rows/count/empty claim; exact pinned detail remains a separate audit route. Consume S4-05's reviewed stored gate form when merged.
+
+One row per canonical Finding; merged-away IDs remain relationship links. Extension changes its revision; separated recurrence gets another Finding ID and link. Include closed and unconfirmed episodes. Intersect iff `episode_start < to && episode_end > from`; boundary touch is excluded. Order selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending; never by money, lifecycle or arrival. Cursor binds tenant/Site/domain/window, basis/selection token and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates it with a typed restart-first-page response; pages cannot splice revision sets. No priority tuple until a producer is reviewed.
+
+Optional amount cell starts **unavailable**. On the stacked S5-01/LR-01 baseline, LR-01 `money_eligibility` is reviewed but S4-05 stored accepted-ID provenance is unmerged: show no current amount. After **both** reviewed contracts enter the baseline, separate review may allow amount only for the exact selected FindingRevision/window/basis and consequence head, open source, current list/consequence, eligible series, priced quantity/price and known source/display unit. Withheld/pending, mixed/unknown source, stale, wrong revision/window, unpriced or closed source means no current amount; old values use explicit audit links. No sum, savings inference or unpriced-as-zero. Money does not gate row/empty delivery.
+

+# S4-04 — Site Finding index and list
+
+Status: implementation-ready (Planner packet, 2026-10-15; build and review pending)
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — owner visual and copy acceptance before merge or registry promotion.
+Size: M, two phases; phase 2 review is the slice review.
+Branch: `task/S4-04-site-finding-index`, **stacked on the independently reviewed S5-01 head** while S5-01 is unmerged. Its same-basis Finding-choice read is needed for parity proof. S5-01 itself is stacked on unmerged LR-01; stacked code is not merged or owner accepted. Resolve any S5-01 High/Medium review finding and rebase on its final reviewed head.
+Depends on: merged S4-01 Finding store/read and S3 assessment; resolved `A-finding-index` (`D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`); reviewed S5-01 for parity. S4-03 is registry queue order, not a technical index gate. LR-01 and S4-05 gate only optional current money.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+`sitefindings:1#a` is a **watermarked assessed zero-row** Site/window list; `sitefindings:1#b` has **persisted canonical Finding rows** and exact revision drill-down. Review both fixtures independently. W1 covers mini-grid dispatch, not the global Findings area or other domains. Authority: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md` (including closed-gate fix), `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, `.agent/A-finding-index-review.md`, `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`, and registry nodes `I-finding-index`, `I-site-findings-ui`, `D-site-finding-index-empty`, `D-site-finding-index-rows`, `I-S4-04`, panel `sitefindings:1` (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:332`). `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:329-334,640-641` and `screen-manifest.json:633-650` guide layout/interaction; illustrative F-108/F-109 are not evidence.
+
+## Verified seam and implementation work
+
+| Existing code on this base | New work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:161-209,213-228` persists immutable revisions, citations and merge edges; `:233-270,274-306` reads exact detail/lineage. | **New** scoped canonical index read over committed Finding/FindingRevision, latest permitted revision per canonical root; no UI seeds or Lab private truth. |
+| `postgres_finding_store.py:400-416` has per-day match state; `:418-430` `findings_over` uses inclusive end and ascending order. `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:83-136` fences selected days/matches. | **New** multi-day discovery and strict half-open filtering. Preserve `findings_over`: `scheduling/health.py:252`, `scheduling/upstream.py:133`, `analytics/finding_read.py:617` consume it. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:56-90,112-135` has access, scope, Performance gate and exact pinned Finding URL. | **New** authenticated index API reusing identity/gate rules; closed gate returns 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` and reason before any unpinned count/row claim. |
+| `frontend/src/App.tsx:248-255`, `frontend/src/shell/operatorSiteRoutes.ts:135-139` mount Site Findings. `frontend/src/sites/FindingsIndexes.tsx:118-176` and `siteAnalyticsClient.ts:242-245,338,402-406` render an older summary, filters and empty copy. | **New** typed index client and selected-window UI for imported Sites. Keep legacy cutover behavior where needed; do not relabel old summary as the index. |
+| **Stacked/unmerged S5-01:** `git show task/S5-01-site-overview:backend/assetops_backend/site_overview/read.py` has `choose`, canonical filtering and `known_finding`. | **New** integration test: first index row's Finding **and revision** equal the S5-01 card on identical Site/day/basis. |
+
+## Read contract (route, DTO and cursor are new)
+
+Add `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/finding-index?domain=mini-grid-dispatch&from=<UTC>&to=<UTC>&limit=<bounded>&cursor=<opaque>` (route spelling is an Architect question below). Resolve tenant/Site via Finding access before discovery: established 401/403/404 and no foreign count. Require explicit domain and UTC half-open `[from,to)`, `from < to`, maximum 31 UTC days; typed 422 otherwise. Echo Site, UTC window and display timezone. The UI persists Site/domain/dates in its URL; date change makes a new read. Server bounds page size.
+
+Return `schema_version`, Site/domain/window, display timezone/access basis, selection token, `knowledge_at`, `built_at`, typed status/reasons, coverage/limitations, watermarks, rows, bounded current `total_count` and `next_cursor`. Watermarks contain accepted-source marker, S3 selected assessment/run/input/configuration and analytic selection, S4 matcher/version/pass/selection. Each row has stable `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id)` key, canonical Finding UUID, exact `finding_revision_id`, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort, cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs, limitations, relation/history links and `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`. Exact links survive reload and newer revisions; lineage comes from the selected immutable revision.
+
+Use one transactionally consistent read or atomically published projection; `knowledge_at` is a selection cutoff, not arbitrary commit-ordered replay. **Complete discovery** means every UTC-day analytic window intersecting the request and neighbours reachable through S4's 20-minute continuity rule has S3 selection and canonical S4 match through its current selection seq on one accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis. Fence before/after read. Moving selection, affected dirty/leased/failed work, watermark mismatch, or late evidence/configuration/model change blocks `CURRENT`. A source-coverage limitation can coexist with a current assessment of available evidence, but cannot assert physical completeness.
+
+`PENDING` means no usable completed selection; `UNASSESSED` no completed discovery basis; `STALE_PROJECTION` keeps prior rows/IDs and a reason; `FAILED` reports failed work with prior rows historical. None authorizes current count, empty/Healthy assertion or swapping stale links for a new head. A closed S3 Performance source gate returns 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` with its reason and no rows/count/empty claim; exact pinned detail remains a separate audit route. Consume S4-05's reviewed stored gate form when merged.
+
+One row per canonical Finding; merged-away IDs remain relationship links. Extension changes its revision; separated recurrence gets another Finding ID and link. Include closed and unconfirmed episodes. Intersect iff `episode_start < to && episode_end > from`; boundary touch is excluded. Order selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending; never by money, lifecycle or arrival. Cursor binds tenant/Site/domain/window, basis/selection token and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates it with a typed restart-first-page response; pages cannot splice revision sets. No priority tuple until a producer is reviewed.
+
+Optional amount cell starts **unavailable**. On the stacked S5-01/LR-01 baseline, LR-01 `money_eligibility` is reviewed but S4-05 stored accepted-ID provenance is unmerged: show no current amount. After **both** reviewed contracts enter the baseline, separate review may allow amount only for the exact selected FindingRevision/window/basis and consequence head, open source, current list/consequence, eligible series, priced quantity/price and known source/display unit. Withheld/pending, mixed/unknown source, stale, wrong revision/window, unpriced or closed source means no current amount; old values use explicit audit links. No sum, savings inference or unpriced-as-zero. Money does not gate row/empty delivery.
+
+## Phases, focused tests and review
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Read and fixtures | New store/service/API, scope/gate, continuity discovery, statuses/cursor. **New** focused real-Postgres tests adjacent to `backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py`, `test_s4_finding_read_postgres.py`, `test_s4_schedule_health_postgres.py`: 401/403/404 and two-tenant/two-Site isolation; 31-day/UTC validation; independently persisted assessed empty `CURRENT` with complete S3/S4 watermark, wrong Site/window and boundary-touch exclusion; unassessed, dirty/leased/stale, failed, moving basis and closed-gate 409/reason; positive revisions, extension/recurrence/merge/closed/unconfirmed, order, cursor/reload, late-revision invalidation and exact lineage. Compare first row to S5-01 card on same day/basis, including UUID tie. Independent phase-1 review. |
+| 2. Site UI and walkthrough | New client/UI with URL-persisted scope; loading/Unknown/stale/failed/closed/assessed-empty/positive, keyboard-operable pinned links and pagination. **New** focused component/browser tests at 1440/390 px: URL change/reload, both fixtures, gate, cursor invalidation, pinned detail/history after newer revision. Keep existing shell/Finding tests. Run `npx.cmd tsc --noEmit`, `npm.cmd test`, `tools/check-all.ps1` and registry checker. Independent phase-2 review is slice review. At final revision run **one** real-Postgres full suite per `backend/TESTING.md:5-21,96-124`: `pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml`, private `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` ending `_test`, keep-awake helper, 2x cap and JUnit zero skipped/failed proof. |
+
+## Reproducible owner walkthrough (new phase-2 fixture/tool)
+
+Add `tools/s404_finding_index_walkthrough.py` (**new**) with `prepare|serve|gate --close|gate --open|late|drop`, private `assetops_s404_owner*` DB and `var/s404_owner*` work path only, API/web ports 8934/5434. Reuse `tools/s4_finding_cases.py`/`tools/s4_finding_walkthrough.py` producers where possible. `prepare` persists two **independent** fixtures: a complete assessed zero-row day with accepted-source and S3/S4 watermarks, and a positive day with at least two canonical persisted Findings/revisions for order, cursor and S5 card parity. Also prepare unassessed, dirty/stale and failed variants. Emit a manifest of Site codes, UTC windows, selection token, exact IDs, accepted IDs and statuses. Stop if completeness cannot be proved; filtered empty is insufficient. `serve` keeps the DB for reload, prints server commands and URLs, and does not install the unattended schedule.
+
+Owner: prepare, serve, open `/sites/{site_code}/findings?from=<empty-start>&to=<next-midnight>&domain=mini-grid-dispatch`; inspect watermark and assessed zero. Select unassessed, dirty and failed variants; see Unknown/stale/failed without zero claims. Open positive window, inspect order/page 2, selected IDs and pinned detail/history after reload. Change date and reload. Run `late`, refresh old cursor (must restart), then reopen old pinned revision and new row. Run `gate --close`; unpinned list gives 409 reason/no count, exact pinned detail stays audit-only. Run `gate --open`, settle matching and reload. Save API responses and 1440/390 screenshots with v6.9 `sitefindings` comparison; identify omitted mock chips, global link, badges and sample money. Owner records visual/copy acceptance or feedback. Hold merge and `sitefindings:1#a/b` promotion until review and owner record.
+
+## Carries and Architect questions
+
+No mock F-108/F-109, global list, Cost/Risks/Opportunities lenses, priority/confidence, “N open”, automatic Healthy or unsupported money. S4-03 Evidence/History and S5-01's other Overview panels stay separate; S5-02 later consumes this order for `mini:3#a/b` without independently ranking. `queue_baseline_nodes` retains the five S4-04 nodes; none leaves. All five registry `task` fields already say `S4-04`, and nodes/panel remain open until reviewed evidence and owner acceptance.
+
+1. **Route and cursor error:** recommend separate `/finding-index` route and 409 `INDEX_BASIS_CHANGED` with fresh-first-page instruction, avoiding `/findings/{finding_id}` collision and changes to `findings_over`.
+2. **Historical rows without a persisted projection:** recommend returning stale rows only from a durable previous selected snapshot; otherwise `PENDING`/`UNASSESSED` with no rows. Never rebuild stale rows from newer heads.
+3. **Continuity-neighbour closure:** recommend bounded expansion from intersected UTC days until no 20-minute episode bridge reaches another day; record inspected days and seq in selection token. Define a typed failure if expansion exceeds the bound.
